{"parentUuid":"cd21b8e9-fda9-4fd3-bf06-4f2e49e2c1cf","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a60157c729ac1afff","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Q22GwJvPKPgZDqjFAnLfdd","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Design → Product Pipeline\n     3→ *\n     4→ * POST /api/designs/:id/create-product\n     5→ * Converts an approved design into a POD provider product:\n     6→ *   1. Validate design is approved\n     7→ *   2. Upload image to provider (if not already uploaded)\n     8→ *   3. Create product with the uploaded image\n     9→ *   4. Publish product\n    10→ *   5. Save provider_product_id to products table\n    11→ */\n    12→\n    13→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n    14→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    15→import { getProvider, initializeProviders } from '@/lib/pod'\n    16→import { verifyCronSecret } from '@/lib/rate-limit'\n    17→import { isEUProvider } from '@/lib/store-config'\n    18→\n    19→const supabase = createClient(\n    20→  process.env.SUPABASE_URL!,\n    21→  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY!\n    22→)\n    23→\n    24→const CRON_SECRET = process.env.CRON_SECRET || process.env.PODCLAW_BRIDGE_AUTH_TOKEN\n    25→\n    26→export async function POST(\n    27→  req: NextRequest,\n    28→  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }\n    29→) {\n    30→  // Auth: require bearer token, timing-safe (called by PodClaw or cron)\n    31→  const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization')\n    32→  if (!verifyCronSecret(authHeader, CRON_SECRET)) {\n    33→    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })\n    34→  }\n    35→\n    36→  const { id: designId } = await params\n    37→\n    38→  // 1. Fetch design\n    39→  const { data: design, error: designError } = await supabase\n    40→    .from('designs')\n    41→    .select('*')\n    42→    .eq('id', designId)\n    43→    .single()\n    44→\n    45→  if (designError || !design) {\n    46→    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Design not found' }, { status: 404 })\n    47→  }\n    48→\n    49→  if (design.moderation_status !== 'approved') {\n    50→    return NextResponse.json(\n    51→      { error: `Design not approved (status: ${design.moderation_status})` },\n    52→      { status: 400 }\n    53→    )\n    54→  }\n    55→\n    56→  // Parse request body for product config\n    57→  // Accepts both legacy (blueprint_id, print_provider_id) and\n    58→  // provider-agnostic (product_template_id, provider_facility_id) field names\n    59→  let body: {\n    60→    blueprint_id?: number\n    61→    print_provider_id?: number\n    62→    product_template_id?: number\n    63→    provider_facility_id?: number\n    64→    variants: Array<{ id: number; price: number; is_enabled: boolean }>\n    65→    title?: string\n    66→    description?: string\n    67→    tags?: string[]\n    68→    has_neck_position?: boolean\n    69→  }\n    70→\n    71→  try {\n    72→    body = await req.json()\n    73→  } catch {\n    74→    return NextResponse.json(\n    75→      { error: 'Request body required with blueprint_id/product_template_id, print_provider_id/provider_facility_id, variants' },\n    76→      { status: 400 }\n    77→    )\n    78→  }\n    79→\n    80→  // Resolve provider-agnostic aliases\n    81→  const blueprintId = body.blueprint_id ?? body.product_template_id\n    82→  const printProviderId = body.print_provider_id ?? body.provider_facility_id\n    83→\n    84→  if (!blueprintId || !printProviderId || !body.variants) {\n    85→    return NextResponse.json(\n    86→      { error: 'Missing required fields: blueprint_id/product_template_id, print_provider_id/provider_facility_id, variants' },\n    87→      { status: 400 }\n    88→    )\n    89→  }\n    90→\n    91→  if (!isEUProvider(printProviderId)) {\n    92→    return NextResponse.json(\n    93→      { error: `Provider ${printProviderId} not approved for EU shipping` },\n    94→      { status: 400 }\n    95→    )\n    96→  }\n    97→\n    98→  try {\n    99→    initializeProviders()\n   100→    const provider = getProvider()\n   101→\n   102→    // 2. Transparency guarantee: prefer bg-removed version\n   103→    let imageUrl = design.bg_removed_url || design.image_url || design.url\n   104→    if (!imageUrl) {\n   105→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Design has no image URL' }, { status: 400 })\n   106→    }\n   107→\n   108→    // If no bg-removed version exists, auto-remove now (defense-in-depth)\n   109→    if (!design.bg_removed_url) {\n   110→      try {\n   111→        const { removeBackground } = await import('@/lib/providers/background-removal')\n   112→        const bgResult = await removeBackground(imageUrl)\n   113→        if (bgResult.success && bgResult.imageUrl) {\n   114→          imageUrl = bgResult.imageUrl\n   115→          await supabase\n   116→            .from('designs')\n   117→            .update({\n   118→              bg_removed_url: imageUrl,\n   119→              bg_removed_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n   120→            })\n   121→            .eq('id', designId)\n   122→        } else {\n   123→          return NextResponse.json(\n   124→            { error: 'Background removal required for product creation but failed. Please try again.' },\n   125→            { status: 422 }\n   126→          )\n   127→        }\n   128→      } catch (bgError) {\n   129→        console.error('Auto bg-removal in create-product failed:', bgError)\n   130→        return NextResponse.json(\n   131→          { error: 'Background removal required but service unavailable.' },\n   132→          { status: 422 }\n   133→        )\n   134→      }\n   135→    }\n   136→\n   137→    // 3. Upload image to provider if not already uploaded\n   138→    let providerUploadId = design.provider_upload_id\n   139→    if (!providerUploadId) {\n   140→\n   141→      const fileName = `design-${designId}.png`\n   142→      const uploadResult = await provider.uploadDesign({ url: imageUrl, fileName })\n   143→      providerUploadId = uploadResult.id\n   144→\n   145→      // Save upload ID to design\n   146→      await supabase\n   147→        .from('designs')\n   148→        .update({\n   149→          provider_upload_id: providerUploadId,\n   150→          pod_upload_url: uploadResult.previewUrl,\n   151→        })\n   152→        .eq('id', designId)\n   153→    }\n   154→\n   155→    // 4. Create product via provider\n   156→    const productTitle = body.title || design.title || `Design ${designId.slice(0, 8)}`\n   157→    const productDescription = body.description || design.description || ''\n   158→\n   159→    // Build placeholders — front design + optional neck label\n   160→    const placeholders: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [\n   161→      {\n   162→        position: 'front',\n   163→        images: [\n   164→          {\n   165→            id: providerUploadId,\n   166→            x: 0.5,\n   167→            y: 0.5,\n   168→            scale: 1,\n   169→            angle: 0,\n   170→          },\n   171→        ],\n   172→      },\n   173→    ]\n   174→\n   175→    // Add neck label if brand config has one and blueprint supports neck position\n   176→    if (body.has_neck_position) {\n   177→      const { data: brandConfig } = await supabase\n   178→        .from('brand_config')\n   179→        .select('neck_label_image_id')\n   180→        .eq('is_active', true)\n   181→        .single()\n   182→\n   183→      if (brandConfig?.neck_label_image_id) {\n   184→        placeholders.push({\n   185→          position: 'neck',\n   186→          images: [\n   187→            {\n   188→              id: brandConfig.neck_label_image_id,\n   189→              x: 0.5,\n   190→              y: 0.5,\n   191→              scale: 1,\n   192→              angle: 0,\n   193→            },\n   194→          ],\n   195→        })\n   196→      }\n   197→    }\n   198→\n   199→    const printifyProduct = await provider.createProduct({\n   200→      title: productTitle,\n   201→      description: productDescription,\n   202→      blueprintId: blueprintId,\n   203→      printProviderId: printProviderId,\n   204→      variants: body.variants.map((v: { id: number; price: number; is_enabled: boolean }) => ({\n   205→        variantId: v.id,\n   206→        priceCents: v.price,\n   207→        isEnabled: v.is_enabled,\n   208→      })),\n   209→      printAreas: placeholders.map((ph: Record<string, unknown>) => ({\n   210→        position: ph.position as string,\n   211→        images: (ph.images as any[]).map((img: any) => ({\n   212→          id: img.id as string,\n   213→          x: img.x as number,\n   214→          y: img.y as number,\n   215→          scale: img.scale as number,\n   216→          angle: img.angle as number,\n   217→        })),\n   218→      })),\n   219→      tags: body.tags || [],\n   220→    })\n   221→\n   222→    // 4. Publish product\n   223→    await provider.publishProduct!(printifyProduct.externalId)\n   224→\n   225→    // 5. Save to products table (before publishingSucceeded so we have the UUID)\n   226→    const { data: product, error: productError } = await supabase\n   227→      .from('products')\n   228→      .insert({\n   229→        title: productTitle,\n   230→        description: productDescription,\n   231→        provider_product_id: printifyProduct.externalId,\n   232→        product_template_id: String(blueprintId),\n   233→        provider_facility_id: String(printProviderId),\n   234→        pod_provider: 'printful',\n   235→        status: 'draft',\n   236→        currency: 'EUR',\n   237→      })\n   238→      .select()\n   239→      .single()\n   240→\n   241→    if (productError) {\n   242→      console.error('Failed to save product to DB:', productError)\n   243→    }\n   244→\n   245→    // 6. Confirm publishing (custom integration requirement)\n   246→    if (product) {\n   247→      try {\n   248→        await provider.confirmPublishing!(\n   249→          printifyProduct.externalId,\n   250→          product.id,\n   251→          `/shop/${product.id}`\n   252→        )\n   253→      } catch (e) {\n   254→        console.error('Failed to confirm publishing:', e)\n   255→        // Non-fatal — webhook or cron will retry\n   256→      }\n   257→    }\n   258→\n   259→    return NextResponse.json({\n   260→      success: true,\n   261→      provider_product_id: printifyProduct.externalId,\n   262→      product_id: product?.id,\n   263→      provider_upload_id: providerUploadId,\n   264→    })\n   265→  } catch (error) {\n   266→    console.error('Design→Product pipeline error:', error instanceof Error ? error.message : error)\n   267→    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Product creation failed' }, { status: 500 })\n   268→  }\n   269→}\n   270→"}]},"uuid":"922ca8e7-329a-47a3-8151-353e92ab4b7a","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:34:02.639Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cd21b8e9-fda9-4fd3-bf06-4f2e49e2c1cf"}
